Boats arriving from Tangyang pay full inrard dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if discharging near the West station; but in the Shippmg table they are all entered as local traders. In the same way boats from Shasi town to Tangyang pay full outward dues but are classed among local clearances. Boats arriving from Tangyang for other ports pay full outward dues. They are entered in the Shipping table first as local entries, then as interport clearances. Boats arriving from other ports for Tangyang have usually no dues to pay, but in the Shipping table they are entered, firstly, as interport entries and, secondly, as local elearances. SCUSTOMSGAZETT. APRIL-JUNE, NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 440. S8 ANG HAI1 0 1STRICOT. NORTH CHANNEL ENTRANCE TO THE YANGTZE RIVER. OHANES iN BuoYAGE Or TSUNGMING COoSSING. NOTICE 1s hereby given that the following alterations have been made in the buoyage of the Tsungming Crossing:- East E%ttraitce Gas-lighted Buoy is moored in 23 feet at low water of spring tides, N. 47&#39; E., distant 3.94 miles from Quarantine BeaCOn, and now marks the iiorthern side of the channel, and its colour is changed to red. First Crossing Buoy isnowm n 33 feet at low water of spring tides, N. 39* 30&#39; E., distant 2.69 miles from Quarantine Beacon. Second Crossing Buoy is now moored in ig feet at low water of spring tides, N. 7&#39; 30&#39; E., distant 1.43 miles from Quarantiie Beacou. SHANGHAI, 18t April 1907.
1907.] NOTnIAowS. 965 No. 441. YANGTZE RIVER-KIUKIANG DISTRICT, BEACONS SHIFTED FURTHER INLAND. NOTICE is hereby given that, owing to the washing away of the river bank, the following beacons have been shifted:- Chuankiangkow Bedcon Light has been shifted to the cliffs on the right bank of the river and bears N. 89&#39; W. from its former position. Eagle Island Beacon Light has been shifted 3oo feet S. 35&#39; E. from its former position. Shansi Point Beacon Light has been shifted 300 feet N. 71 E. from its former position.&#39; Point Otter Beacon Light has been shifted 366 feet S. 28&#39; W. from its former position. Tingliu Beacon Light has been shifted 585 feet S. 150 W. rom its fokmer position. All bearings given are magnetic. SHANGHAI, i8t April 1907.
No. 442. FOOC HOW DISTRICT-MIN RIVER. SPECIAL NAVIGATION RULE FOR THE KIMPAI PASS. NoTicE is hereby given that a careful look out should be kept on board of vessels oaching the Kimpai Pass, and steamers must give one prolonged blast of their whistle n nearing it, Vessels with the tide against them must wait for those going with the tide tpas first. SHANHI, 3rd April 1907. * No. 448. YANGTZE RIVER-CHINKIANG DISTRICT. NORTH TREE BEACON SHIFTED. NOTICE is hereby given that, owing to the Johnson Flats having extended to the eastward, North Tree Beacon has been shifted 1.26 miles S. 37&#39; E. from its former position. SHANGHAI, 22nd April 1907
